PROBLEM
------------------------------
You plan to visit 3 European cities for 14 days in total. You only take direct flights to commute between cities. You would like to visit Florence for 6 days. You want to meet a friend in Florence between day 9 and day 14. You would like to visit Barcelona for 5 days. You would like to visit Helsinki for 5 days.

Here are the cities that have direct flights:
Barcelona and Florence, Helsinki and Barcelona.

Find a trip plan of visiting the cities for 14 days by taking direct flights to commute between them.
================================================================================

    "pddl_domain": "(define (domain trip_planning)\n    (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)\n    (:types\n        city location - object\n        day - object\n        city_count - object\n    )\n    (:constants\n        start_node - location\n    )\n    (:predicates\n        (current_day ?d - day)\n        (next_day ?d1 - day ?d2 - day)\n        (at_location ?l - location)\n        (visited_city ?c - city)\n        (num_cities_visited ?count - city_count)\n        (direct_flight ?c1 - city ?c2 - city)\n        (met_friend_in_florence)\n        (is_meeting_window ?d - day) ; Predicate to mark days 9-14 for friend meeting\n    )\n\n    ;; Action to start the trip, implicitly spending day 1 in the chosen initial city.\n    (:action start_trip\n        :parameters (?c - city ?d1 - day ?d2 - day)\n        :preconditions (and\n            (at_location start_node)\n            (current_day ?d1)\n            (next_day ?d1 ?d2)\n            (num_cities_visited c0)\n        )\n        :effect (and\n            (not (at_location start_node))\n            (at_location ?c)\n            (visited_city ?c)\n            (not (num_cities_visited c0))\n            (num_cities_visited c1)\n            (not (current_day ?d1))\n            (current_day ?d2)\n        )\n    )\n\n    ;; Action to travel to a city that has not been visited before.\n    ;; This action consumes one day and increments the count of unique cities visited.\n    (:action travel_to_new_city\n        :parameters (?from - city ?to - city ?d1 - day ?d2 - day ?count_before - city_count ?count_after - city_count)\n        :preconditions (and\n            (at_location ?from)\n            (direct_flight ?from ?to)\n            (current_day ?d1)\n            (next_day ?d1 ?d2)\n            (not (visited_city ?to))\n            (num_cities_visited ?count_before)\n        )\n        :effect (and\n            (not (at_location ?from))\n            (at_location ?to)\n            (visited_city ?to)\n            (not (current_day ?d1))\n            (current_day ?d2)\n            (not (num_cities_visited ?count_before))\n            (num_cities_visited ?count_after)\n        )\n    )\n\n    ;; Action to travel to a city that has already been visited.\n    ;; This action consumes one day but does not increment the city count.\n    (:action travel_to_visited_city\n        :parameters (?from - city ?to - city ?d1 - day ?d2 - day)\n        :preconditions (and\n            (at_location ?from)\n            (direct_flight ?from ?to)\n            (current_day ?d1)\n            (next_day ?d1 ?d2)\n            (visited_city ?to) ; 'to' city has already been visited\n        )\n        :effect (and\n            (not (at_location ?from))\n            (at_location ?to)\n            (not (current_day ?d1))\n            (current_day ?d2)\n        )\n    )\n\n    ;; Action to stay for one day in the current city.\n    ;; This action consumes one day.\n    (:action stay_in_city\n        :parameters (?c - city ?d1 - day ?d2 - day)\n        :preconditions (and\n            (at_location ?c)\n            (current_day ?d1)\n            (next_day ?d1 ?d2)\n        )\n        :effect (and\n            (not (current_day ?d1))\n            (current_day ?d2)\n        )\n    )\n\n    ;; Action to meet a friend in Florence.\n    ;; This action can only be taken once, in Florence, during the meeting window.\n    (:action meet_friend\n        :parameters (?d - day)\n        :preconditions (and\n            (at_location florence)\n            (current_day ?d)\n            (is_meeting_window ?d)\n            (not (met_friend_in_florence))\n        )\n        :effect (and\n            (met_friend_in_florence)\n        )\n    )\n)",
    "pddl_problem": "(define (problem trip_planning_example0)\n    (:domain trip_planning)\n    (:objects\n        florence barcelona helsinki - city\n        day1 day2 day3 day4 day5 day6 day7 day8 day9 day10 day11 day12 day13 day14 - day\n        c0 c1 c2 c3 - city_count\n    )\n    (:init\n        ; Initial state: agent is at a start_node, it's day 1, and 0 cities have been visited.\n        (at_location start_node)\n        (current_day day1)\n        (num_cities_visited c0)\n\n        ; Day sequencing definition\n        (next_day day1 day2)\n        (next_day day2 day3)\n        (next_day day3 day4)\n        (next_day day4 day5)\n        (next_day day5 day6)\n        (next_day day6 day7)\n        (next_day day7 day8)\n        (next_day day8 day9)\n        (next_day day9 day10)\n        (next_day day10 day11)\n        (next_day day11 day12)\n        (next_day day12 day13)\n        (next_day day13 day14)\n\n        ; Direct flight connections (bidirectional as stated)\n        (direct_flight barcelona florence)\n        (direct_flight florence barcelona)\n        (direct_flight helsinki barcelona)\n        (direct_flight barcelona helsinki)\n\n        ; Days within the meeting window for Florence (Day 9 to Day 14 inclusive)\n        (is_meeting_window day9)\n        (is_meeting_window day10)\n        (is_meeting_window day11)\n        (is_meeting_window day12)\n        (is_meeting_window day13)\n        (is_meeting_window day14)\n    )\n    (:goal (and\n        (current_day day14)           ; Ensure the trip concludes exactly on day 14.\n        (num_cities_visited c3)       ; Ensure all 3 specified cities (Florence, Barcelona, Helsinki) are visited.\n        (met_friend_in_florence)      ; Ensure the friend in Florence is met within the specified window.\n    ))\n)",

Find a trip plan of visiting the cities for 15 days by taking direct flights to commute between them.

    "pddl_domain": "(define (domain trip_planning)\n    (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions)\n    (:types\n        city\n        day\n        count_num\n    )\n    (:predicates\n        (current_day ?d - Action to stay in the current city, within preferred duration\n  (action stay_within_preference\n    :parameters (?c - city ?d_current ?d_next - day)\n    :precondition (and\n                    (at ?c)\n                    (is_day ?d_current)\n                    (next_day ?d_current ?d_next)\n                    ;; Conference on Day 12 must be met if we are on Day 12 and are about to advance past it\n                    (or (not (is_day day12)) (conference_day_12_met))\n                    ;; Conference on Day 16 must be met if we are on Day 16 and are about to advance past it\n                    (or (not (is_day day16)) (conference_day_16_met))\n                    (< (days_spent_in ?c) (preferred_duration ?c)) ; Already met or exceeded preferred days\n                  )\n    :effect (and\n              (not (is_day ?d_current))\n              (is_day ?d_next)\n              (increase (days_spent_in ?c) 1)\n              (increase (actual_trip_days) 1)\n              (increase (total_cost) 1) ;     "pddl_domain": "(define (domain trip-planning)\n  (:requirements :strips :typing :negative-preconditions :action-costs)\n  (:types\n    city - object\n    day - object\n    duration_count - object\n  )\n\n  (:predicates\n    (current_day ?d - day)\n    (next_day ?d1 ?d2 - day)\n    (at ?c - city)\n    (has_flight ?c1 ?c2 - city)\n    (conference_day_12_attended)\n    (conference_day_16_attended)\n    (succeeds ?n1 ?n2 - duration_count) ; n2 is n1 + 1\n    (city_current_stay_duration ?c - city ?n - duration_count) ; Current consecutive days in a city\n    (city_preferred_duration ?c - city ?n - duration_count)    ; Static fact for preferred days\n    (visited_city_at_least_once ?c - city)\n  )\n\n  (:functions\n    (total_cost)\n  )\n\n  ;; Action to stay in the current city, within or at preferred duration\n  ;; This action is blocked if it's a conference day in Dubrovnik and the conference hasn't been attended.\n  (:action stay_preferential\n    :parameters (?c - city ?d_current ?d_next - day ?n_current ?n_next ?n_preferred - duration_count)\n    :precondition (and\n                    (current_day ?d_current)\n                    (next_day ?d_current ?d_next)\n                    (at ?c)\n                    (city_current_stay_duration ?c ?n_current)\n                    (succeeds ?n_current ?n_next)\n                    (city_preferred_duration ?c ?n_preferred)\n                    (not (succeeds ?n_preferred ?n_current)) ; ?n_current is less than or equal to ?n_preferred\n                    ; Block if in Dubrovnik on Day 12 and conference not attended\n                    (not (and (current_day day12) (at ?c) (= ?c dubrovnik) (not (conference_day_12_attended))))\n                    ; Block if in Dubrovnik on Day 16 and conference not attended\n                    (not (and (current_day day16) (at ?c) (= ?c dubrovnik) (not (conference_day_16_attended))))\n                  )\n    :effect (and\n              (not (current_day ?d_current))\n              (current_day ?d_next)\n              (not (city_current_stay_duration ?c ?n_current))\n              (city_current_stay_duration ?c ?n_next)\n              (increase (total_cost) 0) ; No cost for staying within or at preferred duration\n            )\n  )\n\n  ;; Action to stay in the current city, exceeding preferred duration\n  ;; This action is blocked if it's a conference day in Dubrovnik and the conference hasn't been attended.\n  (:action stay_extended\n    :parameters (?c - city ?d_current ?d_next - day ?n_current ?n_next ?n_preferred - duration_count)\n    :precondition (and\n                    (current_day ?d_current)\n                    (next_day ?d_current ?d_next)\n                    (at ?c)\n                    (city_current_stay_duration ?c ?n_current)\n                    (succeeds ?n_current ?n_next)\n                    (city_preferred_duration ?c ?n_preferred)\n                    (succeeds ?n_preferred ?n_current) ; ?n_current is strictly greater than ?n_preferred\n                    ; Block if in Dubrovnik on Day 12 and conference not attended\n                    (not (and (current_day day12) (at ?c) (= ?c dubrovnik) (not (conference_day_12_attended))))\n                    ; Block if in Dubrovnik on Day 16 and conference not attended\n                    (not (and (current_day day16) (at ?c) (= ?c dubrovnik) (not (conference_day_16_attended))))\n                  )\n    :effect (and\n              (not (current_day ?d_current))\n              (current_day ?d_next)\n              (not (city_current_stay_duration ?c ?n_current))\n              (city_current_stay_duration ?c ?n_next)\n              (increase (total_cost) 1) ; Penalty for exceeding preferred duration\n            )\n  )\n\n  ;; Action to travel from one city to another\n  ;; This action is blocked if travelling from Dubrovnik on a conference day and conference not attended.\n  (:action travel\n    :parameters (?from ?to - city ?d_current ?d_next - day ?n_from_curr - duration_count)\n    :precondition (and\n                    (current_day ?d_current)\n                    (next_day ?d_current ?d_next)\n                    (at ?from)\n                    (has_flight ?from ?to)\n                    (not (at ?to)) ; Must not already be at destination\n                    (city_current_stay_duration ?from ?n_from_curr) ; Check duration in current city\n                    (city_current_stay_duration ?to d0) ; Ensure target city currently has 0 days stayed\n                    ; Block if travelling from Dubrovnik on Day 12 and conference not attended\n                    (not (and (current_day day12) (at ?from) (= ?from dubrovnik) (not (conference_day_12_attended))))\n                    ; Block if travelling from Dubrovnik on Day 16 and conference not attended\n                    (not (and (current_day day16) (at ?from) (= ?from dubrovnik) (not (conference_day_16_attended))))\n                  )\n    :effect (and\n              (not (at ?from))\n              (at ?to)\n              (not (current_day ?d_current))\n              (current_day ?d_next)\n              (not (city_current_stay_duration ?from ?n_from_curr))\n              (city_current_stay_duration ?from d0) ; Reset stay duration for the city just left\n              ;(not (city_current_stay_duration ?to d0)) ; Redundant, city_current_stay_duration ?to d1 replaces it\n              (city_current_stay_duration ?to d1) ; Arriving in new city counts as first day\n              (visited_city_at_least_once ?to)\n              (increase (total_cost) 0.5) ; Small cost for travel\n            )\n  )\n\n  ;; Specialized action for staying in Dubrovnik and attending conference on Day 12 (preferential duration)\n  (:action stay_at_dubrovnik_conference_preferential_day12\n    :parameters (?d_current - day ?d_next - day ?n_current ?n_next ?n_preferred - duration_count)\n    :precondition (and\n                    (current_day day12)\n                    (next_day day12 ?d_next)\n                    (at dubrovnik)\n                    (not (conference_day_12_attended))\n                    (city_current_stay_duration dubrovnik ?n_current)\n                    (succeeds ?n_current ?n_next)\n                    (city_preferred_duration dubrovnik ?n_preferred)\n                    (not (succeeds ?n_preferred ?n_current)) ; within or at preferred\n                  )\n    :effect (and\n              (not (current_day day12))\n              (current_day ?d_next)\n              (not (city_current_stay_duration dubrovnik ?n_current))\n              (city_current_stay_duration dubrovnik ?n_next)\n              (conference_day_12_attended)\n              (increase (total_cost) 0)\n            )\n  )\n\n  ;; Specialized action for staying in Dubrovnik and attending conference on Day 12 (extended duration)\n  (:action stay_at_dubrovnik_conference_extended_day12\n    :parameters (?d_current - day ?d_next - day ?n_current ?n_next ?n_preferred - duration_count)\n    :precondition (and\n                    (current_day day12)\n                    (next_day day12 ?d_next)\n                    (at dubrovnik)\n                    (not (conference_day_12_attended))\n                    (city_current_stay_duration dubrovnik ?n_current)\n                    (succeeds ?n_current ?n_next)\n                    (city_preferred_duration dubrovnik ?n_preferred)\n                    (succeeds ?n_preferred ?n_current) ; exceeding preferred\n                  )\n    :effect (and\n              (not (current_day day12))\n              (current_day ?d_next)\n              (not (city_current_stay_duration dubrovnik ?n_current))\n              (city_current_stay_duration dubrovnik ?n_next)\n              (conference_day_12_attended)\n              (increase (total_cost) 1)\n            )\n  )\n\n  ;; Specialized action for staying in Dubrovnik and attending conference on Day 16 (preferential duration)\n  (:action stay_at_dubrovnik_conference_preferential_day16\n    :parameters (?d_current - day ?d_next - day ?n_current ?n_next ?n_preferred - duration_count)\n    :precondition (and\n                    (current_day day16)\n                    (next_day day16 ?d_next) ; This won't exist but is okay as day16 is the end day.\n                    (at dubrovnik)\n                    (not (conference_day_16_attended))\n                    (city_current_stay_duration dubrovnik ?n_current)\n                    (succeeds ?n_current ?n_next)\n                    (city_preferred_duration dubrovnik ?n_preferred)\n                    (not (succeeds ?n_preferred ?n_current)) ; within or at preferred\n                  )\n    :effect (and\n              (not (current_day day16))\n              (current_day ?d_next) ; This will remove day16 and make day17 current, effectively ending the trip.\n              (not (city_current_stay_duration dubrovnik ?n_current))\n              (city_current_stay_duration dubrovnik ?n_next)\n              (conference_day_16_attended)\n              (increase (total_cost) 0)\n            )\n  )\n\n  ;; Specialized action for staying in Dubrovnik and attending conference on Day 16 (extended duration)\n  (:action stay_at_dubrovnik_conference_extended_day16\n    :parameters (?d_current - day ?d_next - day ?n_current ?n_next ?n_preferred - duration_count)\n    :precondition (and\n                    (current_day day16)\n                    (next_day day16 ?d_next)\n                    (at dubrovnik)\n                    (not (conference_day_16_attended))\n                    (city_current_stay_duration dubrovnik ?n_current)\n                    (succeeds ?n_current ?n_next)\n                    (city_preferred_duration dubrovnik ?n_preferred)\n                    (succeeds ?n_preferred ?n_current) ; exceeding preferred\n                  )\n    :effect (and\n              (not (current_day day16))\n              (current_day ?d_next)\n              (not (city_current_stay_duration dubrovnik ?n_current))\n              (city_current_stay_duration dubrovnik ?n_next)\n              (conference_day_16_attended)\n              (increase (total_cost) 1)\n            )\n  )\n)",

You plan to visit 3 European cities for 8 days in total. You only take direct flights to commute between cities. You would like to visit Dubrovnik for 3 days. You would like to visit Berlin for 4 days. During day 1 and day 4, you have to attend a conference in Berlin. You would like to visit Munich for 3 days.

Here are the cities that have direct flights:
Munich and Dubrovnik, Berlin and Munich.

Find a trip plan of visiting the cities for 8 days by taking direct flights to commute between them.
